  • IPAD_089: Gua Tewet
    Coordinates: 1.021195, 117.255167
    Type: In the Tewet Cave, prehistoric rock art was found on the roof of the cave, while a small portion of the wall contains damaged images caused by direct sunlight. Two hundred forty pictures of handprints and anthropomorphic figures (horned animals, lizards, and snakes) were found in the Tewet cave (Fig. 3). The picture of the rock in the cave of Tewet was made using yellow-orange, red, and purple pigments originating from the ochre. While other images created using charcoal produce black hue (Permana, 2015
